# हिन्दी — Hindi / موطأ مالك — Muwatta of Imam Malik

## Narration 1401

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1416)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَنْ غَيَّرَ دِينَهُ فَاضْرِبُوا عُنُقَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَمَعْنَى قَوْلِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِيمَا نُرَى - وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ - ‏&quot;‏ مَنْ غَيَّرَ دِينَهُ فَاضْرِبُوا عُنُقَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ أَنَّهُ مَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ إِلَى غَيْرِهِ مِثْلُ الزَّنَادِقَةِ وَأَشْبَاهِهِمْ فَإِنَّ أُولَئِكَ إِذَا ظُهِرَ عَلَيْهِمْ قُتِلُوا وَلَمْ يُسْتَتَابُوا لأَنَّهُ لاَ تُعْرَفُ تَوْبَتُهُمْ وَأَنَّهُمْ كَانُوا يُسِرُّونَ الْكُفْرَ وَيُعْلِنُونَ الإِسْلاَمَ فَلاَ أَرَى أَنْ يُسْتَتَابَ هَؤُلاَءِ وَلاَ يُقْبَلُ مِنْهُمْ قَوْلُهُمْ وَأَمَّا مَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ إِلَى غَيْرِهِ وَأَظْهَرَ ذَلِكَ فَإِنَّهُ يُسْتَتَابُ فَإِنْ تَابَ وَإِلاَّ قُتِلَ وَذَلِكَ لَوْ أَنَّ قَوْمًا كَانُوا عَلَى ذَلِكَ رَأَيْتُ أَنْ يُدْعَوْا إِلَى الإِسْلاَمِ وَيُسْتَتَابُوا فَإِنْ تَابُوا قُبِلَ ذَلِكَ مِنْهُمْ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَتُوبُوا قُتِلُوا وَلَمْ يُعْنَ بِذَلِكَ فِيمَا نُرَى وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ مَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الْيَهُودِيَّةِ إِلَى النَّصْرَانِيَّةِ وَلاَ مِنَ النَّصْرَانِيَّةِ إِلَى الْيَهُودِيَّةِ وَلاَ مَنْ يُغَيِّرُ دِينَهُ مِنْ أَهْلِ الأَدْيَانِ كُلِّهَا إِلاَّ الإِسْلاَمَ فَمَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ إِلَى غَيْرِهِ وَأَظْهَرَ ذَلِكَ فَذَلِكَ الَّذِي عُنِيَ بِهِ وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about someone who consumed an animal without the permission of its owner, is that he must pay its price on the day he consumed it. He is not obliged to replace it with a similar animal nor does he compensate the owner with any kind of animal. He must pay its price on the day it was consumed, and giving the value is more equitable in compensation for animals and goods.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say about someone who consumes some food without the permission of its owner, &quot;He returns to the owner a like weight of the same kind of food. Food is in the position of gold and silver. Gold and silver are returned with gold and silver. The animal is not in the position of gold in that. What distinguishes between them is the sunna and the behaviour which is in force. Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;If a man is entrusted with some wealth and then trades with it for himself and makes a profit, the profit is his because he is responsible for the property until he returns it to its owner. &quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If someone changes his deen - strike his neck!&quot; The meaning of the statement of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, in our opinion and Allah knows best, is that &quot;if someone changes his deen, strike his neck!&quot; refers to those who leave Islam for other than it - like the heretics and their like, about whom it is known. They are killed without being called to tawba because their tawba is not recognised. They were hiding their kufr and publishing their Islam, so I do not think that one calls such people to tawba, and one does not accept their word. As for the one who goes out of Islam to something else and divulges it, one calls him to tawba. If he does not turn in tawba, he is killed. If there are people in that situation, I think that one should call them to Islam and call them to tawba. If they turn in tawba, that is accepted from them. If they do not turn in tawba, they are killed. That does not refer as we see it, and Allah knows best, to those who come out of Judaism to Christianity or from Christianity to Judaism, nor to someone who changes his deen from the various forms of deen except for Islam. Whoever comes out of Islam to other than it and divulges that, that is the one who is referred to, and Allah knows best</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलम से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया जो शख़्स अपना दीन बदल डाले (मुर्तद हो जाए) तो उसकी गर्दन मारो। मालिक ने कहा रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के इस फरमान का हमारे नज़दीक मतलब ये है के जो मुसलमान इस्लाम से बाहर हो जाए जैसे ज़नादिक़ा या उनकी मानिंद, तो जब मुसलमान उन पर ग़लबा पाएँ तो उन्हें क़त्ल कर दें। ये भी ज़रूरी नहीं के पहले उनसे तौबा करने को कहें क्योंकि उनकी तौबा का ऐतबार नहीं हो सकता, वो कुफ़्र को अपने दिल में रखते हैं और ज़ाहिर में अपने तईं मुसलमान कहते हैं। लेकिन अगर कोई मुसलमान शख़्स किसी शुब्हे की वजह से एलानिया दीन-ए-इस्लाम से फिर जाए, तो उस से तौबा करवाएँ (और जो शुबा हो उसे दूर करें), अगर तौबा करे तो बेहतर वरना क़त्ल किया जाए। और जो काफ़िर एक कुफ़्र के दीन को छोड़ कर दूसरा कुफ़्र का दीन इख़्तियार करे, मिसाल के तौर पर पहले यहूदी था फिर नस्रानी हो जाए, तो उसे क़त्ल नहीं करेंगे, बल्कि जो दीन-ए-इस्लाम को छोड़ कर और कोई दीन इख़्तियार करेगा उसी के लिए ये सज़ा है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے جو شخص اپنا دین بدل ڈالے تو اس کی گردن مارو۔ کہا مالک نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے یہ جو فرمایا جو شخص اپنا دین بدل ڈالے اس کی گردن مارو ہمارے نزدیک اس کے معنی یہ ہیں جو مسلمان اسلام سے باہر ہوجائیں جیسے زنادقہ یا ان کی مانند تو جب مسلمان ان پر غلبہ پائیں تو ان کو قتل کردیں یہ بھی ضروری نہیں کہ پہلے ان سے توبہ کرنے کو کہیں کیونکہ ان کی توبہ کا اعتبار نہیں ہوسکتا وہ کفر کو اپنے دل میں رکھتے ہیں اور ظاہر میں اپنے تئیں مسلمان کہتے ہیں لیکن اگر مسلمان شخص (کسی شبہ کی وجہ سے) علانیہ دین اسلام سے پھر جائے تو اس سے توبہ کرائیں (اور جو شبہ ہوا ہو اس کو دور کردیں) اگر توبہ کرے تو بہتر۔ ورنہ قتل کیا جائے اور جو کافر ایک کفر کے دین کو چھوڑ کر دوسرا کفر کا دین اختیار کرے مثلا پہلے یہودی تھا پھر نصرانی ہوجائے تو اس کو قتل نہ کریں گے بلکہ جو دین اسلام کو چھوڑ کر اور کوئی دین اختیار کرے گا اسی کے لئے یہ سزاہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya jo shakhs apna deen badal dale (murtad ho jaye) toh uski gardan maro. Malik ne kaha Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke is farman ka hamare nazdeek matlab ye hai ke jo Musalman Islam se bahar ho jaye jaise Zanadiqa ya unki manind, toh jab Musalman un par ghalba payein toh unhein qatl kar dein. Ye bhi zaroori nahi ke pehle unse tauba karne ko kahein kyunke unki tauba ka aitbar nahi ho sakta, woh kufr ko apne dil mein rakhte hain aur zahir mein apne tayein Musalman kehte hain. Lekin agar koi Musalman shakhs kisi shubhe ki wajah se alaniya deen-e-Islam se phir jaye, toh us se tauba karwayein (aur jo shuba ho use door karein), agar tauba kare toh behtar warna qatl kiya jaye. Aur jo kafir ek kufr ke deen ko chhod kar dusra kufr ka deen ikhtiyar kare, misal ke taur par pehle Yahudi tha phir Nasrani ho jaye, toh use qatl nahi karenge, balki jo deen-e-Islam ko chhod kar aur koi deen ikhtiyar karega usi ke liye ye saza hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدٍ الْقَارِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَدِمَ عَلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ رَجُلٌ مِنْ قِبَلِ أَبِي مُوسَى الأَشْعَرِيِّ فَسَأَلَهُ عَنِ النَّاسِ، فَأَخْبَرَهُ ثُمَّ، قَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ هَلْ كَانَ فِيكُمْ مِنْ مُغَرِّبَةِ خَبَرٍ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ رَجُلٌ كَفَرَ بَعْدَ إِسْلاَمِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَمَا فَعَلْتُمْ بِهِ قَالَ قَرَّبْنَاهُ فَضَرَبْنَا عُنُقَهُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ أَفَلاَ حَبَسْتُمُوهُ ثَلاَثًا وَأَطْعَمْتُمُوهُ كُلَّ يَوْمٍ رَغِيفًا وَاسْتَتَبْتُمُوهُ لَعَلَّهُ يَتُوبُ وَيُرَاجِعُ أَمْرَ اللَّهِ ثُمَّ قَالَ عُمَرُ اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي لَمْ أَحْضُرْ وَلَمْ آمُرْ وَلَمْ أَرْضَ إِذْ بَلَغَنِي ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al- Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Abd ar-Rahman ibn Muhammad ibn Abdullah ibn Abd al-Qari that his father said, &quot;A man came to Umar ibn al- Khattab from Abu Musa al-Ashari. Umar asked after various people, and he informed him. Then Umar inquired, &#x27;Do you have any recent news?&#x27; He said, &#x27;Yes. A man has become a kafir after his Islam.&#x27; Umar asked, &#x27;What have you done with him?&#x27; He said, &#x27;We let him approach and struck off his head.&#x27; Umar said, &#x27;Didn&#x27;t you imprison him for three days and feed him a loaf of bread every day and call on him to tawba that he might turn in tawba and return to the command of Allah?&#x27; Then Umar said, &#x27;O Allah! I was not present and I did not order it and I am not pleased since it has come to me</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1417]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1417" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1417 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुहम्मद बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्दुल क़ारी से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास एक शख़्स आया अबू मूसा अशअरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास से। हज़रत उमर ने उस से वहाँ के लोगों का हाल पूछा, उसने बयान किया। फिर हज़रत उमर ने कहा तुम को कोई नादिर चीज़ मालूम है? वो शख़्स बोला हाँ, एक शख़्स काफ़िर हो गया था बाद इस्लाम के। हज़रत उमर ने पूछा तुम ने उस से क्या किया? वो शख़्स बोला हम ने उसे पकड़ा और उसकी गर्दन मार दी। हज़रत उमर ने कहा तुम ने उसको तीन दिन तक क़ैद किया होता और हर रोज़ रोटी दी होती, फिर तौबा करवाई होती, शायद वो तौबा करता और फिर अल्लाह के हुक्म मान लेता। फिर हज़रत उमर ने फरमाया या अल्लाह! मैं उस वक़्त वहाँ मौजूद न था, न मैंने हुक्म दिया, न मैं ख़ुश हुआ जब मुझे मालूम हुआ।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1417" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">محمد بن عبداللہ بن عبدالقاری سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر کے پاس ایک شخص آیا ابوموسیٰ اشعری کے پاس سے حضرت عمر نے اس سے وہاں کے لوگوں کا حال پوچھا اس نے بیان کیا پھر حضرت عمر نے کہا تم کو کوئی نادر چیز معلوم ہے وہ شخص بولا ہاں ایک شخص کافر ہو گیا تھا بعد اسلام کے حضرت عمر نے پوچھا تم نے اس سے کیا کیا وہ شخص بولا ہم نے اسے پکڑا اور اس کی گردن ماردی حضرت عمر نے کہا تم نے اس کو تین دن تک قید کیا ہوتا اور ہر روز روٹی دی ہوتی پھر توبہ کروائی ہوتی شائدوہ توبہ کرتا اور پھر اللہ کے حکم مان لیتا پھر حضرت عمر نے فرمایا یا اللہ میں اس وقت وہاں موجود نہ تھا مں نے حکم کیا نہ میں خوش ہوا جب کہ مجھے معلوم ہوا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Muhammad bin Abdullah bin Abdul Qari se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas ek shakhs aaya Abu Musa Ash&#x27;ari (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas se. Hazrat Umar ne us se wahan ke logon ka haal pucha, usne bayan kiya. Phir Hazrat Umar ne kaha tum ko koi nadir cheez maloom hai? Woh shakhs bola haan, ek shakhs kafir ho gaya tha baad Islam ke. Hazrat Umar ne pucha tum ne us se kya kiya? Woh shakhs bola hum ne use pakda aur uski gardan maar di. Hazrat Umar ne kaha tum ne usko teen din tak qaid kiya hota aur har roz roti di hoti, phir tauba karwayi hoti, shayad woh tauba karta aur phir Allah ke hukum maan leta. Phir Hazrat Umar ne farmaya ya Allah! Main us waqt wahan maujood na tha, na maine hukum diya, na main khush hua jab mujhe maloom hua.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1417" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1403

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1418)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سُهَيْلِ بْنِ أَبِي صَالِحٍ السَّمَّانِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ سَعْدَ بْنَ عُبَادَةَ، قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَرَأَيْتَ إِنْ وَجَدْتُ مَعَ امْرَأَتِي رَجُلاً أَأُمْهِلُهُ حَتَّى آتِيَ بِأَرْبَعَةِ شُهَدَاءَ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ نَعَمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Suhayl ibn Abi Salih as- Samman from his father from Abu Hurayra that Sad ibn Ubada said to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &quot;What do you think if I find a man with my wife? Shall I grant him a respite until I bring four witnesses?&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, replied, &quot;Yes</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के सा&#x27;आद बिन उबड़ा ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से कहा अगर मैं अपनी औरत के साथ किसी मर्द को पौन तोह क्या मैं उसको मोहलत दूँ यहाँ तक के चार गवाह लॉन? फ़रमाया आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ سعد بن عبادہ نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے کہا اگر میں اپنی عورت کے ساتھ کسی مرد کو پاؤں کیا میں اس کو مہلت دوں یہاں تک کہ چار گواہ لاؤں فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ہاں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

## Narration 1404

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1419)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنْ أَهْلِ الشَّامِ - يُقَالُ لَهُ ابْنُ خَيْبَرِيٍّ - وَجَدَ مَعَ امْرَأَتِهِ رَجُلاً فَقَتَلَهُ أَوْ قَتَلَهُمَا مَعًا فَأَشْكَلَ عَلَى مُعَاوِيَةَ بْنِ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ الْقَضَاءُ فِيهِ فَكَتَبَ إِلَى أَبِي مُوسَى الأَشْعَرِيِّ يَسْأَلُ لَهُ عَلِيَّ بْنَ أَبِي طَالِبٍ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَسَأَلَ أَبُو مُوسَى عَنْ ذَلِكَ عَلِيَّ بْنَ أَبِي طَالِبٍ فَقَالَ لَهُ عَلِيٌّ إِنَّ هَذَا الشَّىْءَ مَا هُوَ بِأَرْضِي عَزَمْتُ عَلَيْكَ لَتُخْبِرَنِّي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ أَبُو مُوسَى كَتَبَ إِلَىَّ مُعَاوِيَةُ بْنُ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ أَنْ أَسْأَلَكَ عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَلِيٌّ أَنَا أَبُو حَسَنٍ إِنْ لَمْ يَأْتِ بِأَرْبَعَةِ شُهَدَاءَ فَلْيُعْطَ بِرُمَّتِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1419" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Ali ibn Abi Talib (companion). The source episode and Ali’s exact response are retained. The final explanation of the pronouncement remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Said ibn al-Musayyab that a Syrian man called Ibn Khaybari found a man with his wife and killed him, or killed them both. Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan found it difficult to make a decision and he wrote to Abu Musa al-Ashari to ask Ali ibn Abi Talib for him about that. So Abu Musa asked Ali ibn Abi Talib and AIi said to him, &quot;Is this thing in my land? I adjure you, you must tell me.&quot; Abu Musa explained to him how Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan had written him to ask Ali about it. Ali said, &quot;I am Abu Hasan. If he does not bring four witnesses, then let him be completely handed over,&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1419]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1419" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के शाम वालों में से एक शख़्स ने अपनी औरत के साथ एक मर्द को पाया तो उस मर्द को या मर्द और औरत दोनों को मार डाला। मुआविया बिन अबी सुफ़ियान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को इस फ़ैसले में दुश्वारी हुई, उन्होंने अबू मूसा अशअरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को लिखा के तुम हज़रत अली (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से इस मसले को पूछो। अबू मूसा ने हज़रत अली (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पूछा, हज़रत अली ने कहा ये वाक़िया मेरे मुल्क में नहीं हुआ, मैं तुम को क़सम देता हूँ तुम सच बयान करो कहाँ ये अमर हुआ। अबू मूसा ने कहा मुझे मुआविया बिन सुफ़ियान ने लिखा है के मैं तुम से इस मसले को पूछूँ। हज़रत अली ने कहा मैं अबुल हसन हूँ, अगर चार गवाह न लाए तो क़त्ल पर राज़ी हो जाए।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1419" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے شام والوں میں سے اپنی عورت کے ساتھ ایک مرد کو پایا تو مار ڈالا اس مرد کو یا مرد عورت دونوں کو معاویہ بن ابی سفیان ان کو اس فیصلہ دشوار ہوا انہوں نے ابوموسیٰ اشعری کو لکھا کہ تم حضرت علی رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے اس مسئلہ کو پوچھو ابوموسیٰ نے حضرت علی رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے پوچھا حضرت علی نے کہا یہ واقعہ میرے ملک میں نہیں ہوا میں تم کو قسم دیتا ہوں تم سچ بیان کرو کہاں یہ امر ہوا ابوموسیٰ نے کہا مجھے معاویہ بن سفیان نے لکھا ہے کہ میں تم سے اس مسئلہ کو پوچھوں حضرت علی نے کہا میں ابوا لحسن ہوں اگر چار گواہ نہ لائے تو قتل پر راضی ہو جائے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1419" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyab se riwayat hai ke Shaam walon mein se ek shakhs ne apni aurat ke saath ek mard ko paya toh us mard ko ya mard aur aurat dono ko maar dala. Muawiya bin Abi Sufiyan (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko is faisle mein dushwari hui, unhone Abu Musa Ash&#x27;ari (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko likha ke tum Hazrat Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) se is masle ko pucho. Abu Musa ne Hazrat Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) se pucha, Hazrat Ali ne kaha ye waqia mere mulk mein nahi hua, main tum ko qasam deta hoon tum sach bayan karo kahan ye amar hua. Abu Musa ne kaha mujhe Muawiya bin Sufiyan ne likha hai ke main tum se is masle ko puchun. Hazrat Ali ne kaha main Abul Hassan hoon, agar chaar gawah na laye toh qatl par raazi ho jaye.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1419" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1405

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1420)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1420]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1420" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1420</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1420 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سُنَيْنٍ أَبِي جَمِيلَةَ، رَجُلٌ مِنْ بَنِي سُلَيْمٍ أَنَّهُ وَجَدَ مَنْبُوذًا فِي زَمَانِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ قَالَ فَجِئْتُ بِهِ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَقَالَ مَا حَمَلَكَ عَلَى أَخْذِ هَذِهِ النَّسَمَةِ فَقَالَ وَجَدْتُهَا ضَائِعَةً فَأَخَذْتُهَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ عَرِيفُهُ يَا أَمِيرَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ إِنَّهُ رَجُلٌ صَالِحٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ أَكَذَلِكَ قَالَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ اذْهَبْ فَهُوَ حُرٌّ وَلَكَ وَلاَؤُهُ وَعَلَيْنَا نَفَقَتُهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1420" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that Malik related from Ibn Shihab that Sunayn Abi Jamila, a man from the Banu Sulaym, found an abandoned child in the time of Umar ibn al-Khattab. Sunayn took him to Umar ibn al-Khattab. He asked, &quot;What has induced you to take this person?&quot; He answered, &quot;I found him lost, so I took him.&#x27;&#x27; Umar&#x27;s advisor said to him,&#x27; &#x27;Amir al-Muminin! He is a man who does good.&quot; Umar inquired of him, &quot;Is it so?&quot; He replied, &quot;Yes.&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;Go, he is free, and you have his wala&#x27; inheritance, and we will provide for him.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1420]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1420" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1420 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about an abandoned child is that he is free, and his wala&#x27; inheritance belongs to the muslims, and they inherit from him and pay his blood money</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुनैं बिन आबि जमीला ने एक मंबुज (ला-वारिस बचा) पाया हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के ज़माने में. उन्होंने कहा मैं उसको हज़रात उम्र के पास ले आया. हज़रात उम्र ने पूछा तुम ने उसको क्यों उठाया? मैंने कहा ये पड़े पड़े मर जाता इस वास्ते मैंने उठा लिया. इतने में हज़रात उम्र के आरीफ ने कहा ऐ अमीर-उल-मोमिनीन आदमी है. हज़रात उमर ने कहा नेक है? उसने कहा हाँ. हज़रात उमर ने कहा जाओ (हक़-इ-विरासत) मिलेगी और हम उसका खर्च देंगे. मलिक ने कहा मानबूझ आज़ाद रहेगा और वाला उसकी मुसलमानों को मिलेगी वही उसके वारिस होंगे वही उसकी तरफ से दियत भी देंगे.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1420" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سنین بن ابی جمیلہ نے ایک منبوذ پایا حضرت عمر کے زمانے میں انہوں نے کہا میں اس کو حضرت عمر کے پاس لے آیا حضرت عمر نے پوچھا تو نے اس کو کیوں اٹھایا میں نے کہا یہ پڑے پڑے مر جاتا اس واسطے میں نے اٹھا لیا اتنے میں حضرت عمر کے عریف نے کہا اے امیر المومنین میں اس شخص کو جانتا ہوں نیک آدمی ہے حضرت عمر نے کہا نیک ہے اس نے کہا ہاں حضرت عمر نے کہا جادہ مبنوذ آزاد ہے تجھ کو اس کی ولا ملے گی اور ہم اس کا خرچ دیں گے ۔ کہا مالک نے منبوذ آزاد رہے گا اور ولاء اس کی مسلمانوں کو ملے گی وہی اس کے وارث ہوں گے وہی اس کی طرف سے دیت بھی دیں گے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1420" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1420 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sunain bin Abi Jamila ne ek manbooz (la-waris bacha) paya Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke zamane mein. Unhone kaha main usko Hazrat Umar ke paas le aaya. Hazrat Umar ne pucha tum ne usko kyun uthaya? Maine kaha ye pade pade mar jata is waste maine utha liya. Itne mein Hazrat Umar ke areef ne kaha ae Ameer-ul-Momineen, main is shakhs ko janta hoon, nek aadmi hai. Hazrat Umar ne kaha nek hai? Usne kaha haan. Hazrat Umar ne kaha jao, manbooz azad hai, tujh ko uski wala (haq-e-wirasat) milegi aur hum uska kharch denge. Malik ne kaha manbooz azad rahega aur wala uski Musalmanon ko milegi, wahi uske waris honge wahi uski taraf se diyat bhi denge.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1420" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1406

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1421)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ كَانَ عُتْبَةُ بْنُ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ عَهِدَ إِلَى أَخِيهِ سَعْدِ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ أَنَّ ابْنَ وَلِيدَةِ زَمْعَةَ مِنِّي فَاقْبِضْهُ إِلَيْكَ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَلَمَّا كَانَ عَامُ الْفَتْحِ أَخَذَهُ سَعْدٌ وَقَالَ ابْنُ أَخِي قَدْ كَانَ عَهِدَ إِلَىَّ فِيهِ ‏.‏ فَقَامَ إِلَيْهِ عَبْدُ بْنُ زَمْعَةَ فَقَالَ أَخِي وَابْنُ وَلِيدَةِ أَبِي وُلِدَ عَلَى فِرَاشِهِ ‏.‏ فَتَسَاوَقَا إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ سَعْدٌ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ابْنُ أَخِي قَدْ كَانَ عَهِدَ إِلَىَّ فِيهِ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ عَبْدُ بْنُ زَمْعَةَ أَخِي وَابْنُ وَلِيدَةِ أَبِي وُلِدَ عَلَى فِرَاشِهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ هُوَ لَكَ يَا عَبْدُ بْنَ زَمْعَةَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ الْوَلَدُ لِلْفِرَاشِ وَلِلْعَاهِرِ الْحَجَرُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَالَ لِسَوْدَةَ بِنْتِ زَمْعَةَ ‏&quot;‏ احْتَجِبِي مِنْهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ لِمَا رَأَى مِنْ شَبَهِهِ بِعُتْبَةَ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ قَالَتْ فَمَا رَآهَا حَتَّى لَقِيَ اللَّهَ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1421" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az-Zubayr that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;&#x27;Utba ibn Abi Waqqas disclosed to his brother, Sad ibn Abi Waqqas, that he was the father of the son of the slave-girl of Zama, and made him promise to look after him (after his death). In the year of the conquest, Sad took him and said, &#x27;He is the son of my brother. He covenanted with me about him.&#x27; Abd ibn Zama stood up and said, &#x27;He is my brother and the son of my father&#x27;s slave-girl. He was born on his bed.&#x27; They went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. Sad said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah! He is the son of my brother, he made a covenant with me about him.&#x27; Abd ibn Zama said, &#x27;He is my brother and the son of my father&#x27;s slave-girl and was born on my father&#x27;s bed.&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;He is yours, Abd ibn Zama.&#x27; Then the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;A child belongs to the household (where he was born) and the adulterer is stoned.&#x27; Then he told Sawda bint Zama, &#x27;Veil yourself from him,&#x27; since he saw in him a resemblance to Utba ibn Abi Waqqas.&quot; A&#x27;isha added, &quot;He did not see her until he met Allah, the Mighty, the Majestic</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के उत्बाह बिन अबी वक़्क़ास ने मरते वक़्त अपने भाई साअद बिन अबी वक़्क़ास से कहा के ज़माअह की लौंडी का लड़का मेरे नुत्फ़ा से है तो उसको अपने पास रखियो। जब मक्का फ़तह हुआ तो साअद ने उस लड़के को ले लिया और कहा ये मेरे भाई का बेटा है, उसने वसीयत की थी इसके लेने की। अब्द-ए-ज़माअह ने कहा ये लड़का मेरा भाई है, मेरे बाप की लौंडी का बेटा है। दोनों ने झगड़ा किया तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास गए। साअद ने कहा या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ये बेटा है मेरे भाई का, उसने मुझे वसीयत की थी और मेरे बाप की लौंडी से पैदा हुआ है। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अब्द-ए-ज़माअह से फरमाया के ये लड़का तेरा है, फिर फरमाया लड़का माँ के ख़ावंद या मालिक का होता है और ज़िना करने वाले के लिए पत्थर (सज़ा) है। फिर सौदा बिंत ज़माअह से कहा के तू इस लड़के से पर्दा किया कर क्योंकि वो लड़का मुशाबाह था उत्बाह बिन अबी वक़्क़ास के, सो उस लड़के ने न देखा सौदा को यहाँ तक के उसका इंतेक़ाल हो गया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ عتبہ بن ابی وقاص نے مرتے وقت اپنے بھائی سعد بن ابی وقاص سے کہا کہ زمعہ کی لونڈی کا لڑکا میرے نطفہ سے ہے تو اس کو اپنے پاس رکھیوں تو جب مکہ فتح ہوا تو سعد نے اس لڑکے کو لے لیا اور کہا میرے بھائی کا بیٹا ہے اس نے وصیت کی تھی اس کے لینے کی عبد زمعہ نے کہا یہ لڑکا میرا بھائی ہے میرے باپ کی لونڈی کا بیٹا ہے دونوں نے جھگڑا کیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس سعد نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم یہ بیٹا ہے میرے بھائی کا اس نے مجھے وصیت کی تھی اور میرے باپ کی لونڈی سے پیدا ہوا ہے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا عبد زمعہ سے کہ یہ لڑکا تیرا ہے پھر فرمایا لڑکا ماں کے خاوند یا مالک کا ہوتا ہے اور زنا کرنے والے کے لئے پتھر ہیں پھر سودہ بنت زمعہ سے کہا کہ تو اس لڑکے سے پردہ کیا کر کیونکہ وہ لڑکا مشابہ تھا عتبہ بن ابی وقاص کے سو اس لڑکے نے نہ دیکھا سودہ کر یہاں تک کہ انتقال ہوا اس کا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Utbah bin Abi Waqqas ne marte waqt apne bhai Sa&#x27;ad bin Abi Waqqas se kaha ke Zama&#x27;ah ki laundi ka ladka mere nutfa se hai toh usko apne paas rakhiyo. Jab Makkah fatah hua toh Sa&#x27;ad ne us ladke ko le liya aur kaha ye mere bhai ka beta hai, usne wasiyat ki thi iske lene ki. Abd-e-Zama&#x27;ah ne kaha ye ladka mera bhai hai, mere baap ki laundi ka beta hai. Dono ne jhagda kiya toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas gaye. Sa&#x27;ad ne kaha ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ye beta hai mere bhai ka, usne mujhe wasiyat ki thi aur mere baap ki laundi se paida hua hai. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Abd-e-Zama&#x27;ah se farmaya ke ye ladka tera hai, phir farmaya ladka maa ke khawand ya maalik ka hota hai aur zina karne wale ke liye patthar (saza) hai. Phir Saudah bint Zama&#x27;ah se kaha ke tu is ladke se parda kiya kar kyunke wo ladka mushabah tha Utbah bin Abi Waqqas ke, so us ladke ne na dekha Saudah ko yahan tak ke uska inteqal ho gaya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1421" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1407

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1422]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْهَادِي، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ التَّيْمِيِّ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي أُمَيَّةَ، أَنَّ امْرَأَةً، هَلَكَ عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا فَاعْتَدَّتْ أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ وَعَشْرًا ثُمَّ تَزَوَّجَتْ حِينَ حَلَّتْ فَمَكَثَتْ عِنْدَ زَوْجِهَا أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ وَنِصْفَ شَهْرٍ ثُمَّ وَلَدَتْ وَلَدًا تَامًّا فَجَاءَ زَوْجُهَا إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَدَعَا عُمَرُ نِسْوَةً مِنْ نِسَاءِ الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ قُدَمَاءَ فَسَأَلَهُنَّ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَتِ امْرَأَةٌ مِنْهُنَّ أَنَا أُخْبِرُكَ عَنْ هَذِهِ الْمَرْأَةِ هَلَكَ عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا حِينَ حَمَلَتْ مِنْهُ فَأُهْرِيقَتْ عَلَيْهِ الدِّمَاءُ فَحَشَّ وَلَدُهَا فِي بَطْنِهَا فَلَمَّا أَصَابَهَا زَوْجُهَا الَّذِي نَكَحَهَا وَأَصَابَ الْوَلَدَ الْمَاءُ تَحَرَّكَ الْوَلَدُ فِي بَطْنِهَا وَكَبِرَ ‏.‏ فَصَدَّقَهَا عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَفَرَّقَ بَيْنَهُمَا وَقَالَ عُمَرُ أَمَا إِنَّهُ لَمْ يَبْلُغْنِي عَنْكُمَا إِلاَّ خَيْرٌ وَأَلْحَقَ الْوَلَدَ بِالأَوَّلِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: historical report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion); unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The source records Umar’s inquiry and an unnamed woman’s historical explanation; that explanation is not asserted as present medical knowledge or Prophetic speech. The explicit explanatory parenthesis remains context. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yazid ibn Abdullah ibn al-Hadi from Muhammad ibn Ibrahim ibn al-Harith at-Taymi from Sulayman ibn Yasar from Abdullah ibn Abi Umayya that a woman&#x27;s husband died, and she did the idda of four months and ten days. Then she married when she was free to marry. She stayed with her husband for four and a half months, then gave birth to a fully developed child. Her husband went to Umar ibn al-Khattab and mentioned that to him, so Umar called some of the old women of the Jahiliyya and asked them about that. One of the women said, &quot;I will tell you what happened with this woman. When her husband died, she was pregnant by him, but then the blood flowed from her because of his death and the child became dry in her womb. When her new husband had intercourse with her and the water reached the child, the child moved in the womb and grew.&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab believed her and separated them</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr">. Umar said, &quot;Only good has reached me about you two,&quot; and he connected the child to the first husband</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1422]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1422" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1422]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1422" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1422</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1422 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्ला बिन उमय्याह से रिवायत है के एक औरत का ख़ावंद मर गया तो उसने चार महीने दस दिन तक इद्दत की, फिर दूसरे शख़्स से निकाह कर लिया। अभी उसके पास सड़हे चार महीने रही थी के एक लड़का जना (पैदा किया) जो बिल्कुल पूरा था। तो उसका ख़ावंद हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आया और उसने ये हाल बयान किया। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने अपनी पुरानी औरतों को जो जाहिलियत के ज़माने में थीं बुलवाया और उनसे पूछा। उन में से एक औरत बोली मैं तुमको इस औरत का हाल बताती हूँ, ये हामिला हो गई थी अपने पहले ख़ावंद से जो मर गया, तो हैज़ का ख़ून बच्चे पर पड़ते पड़ते वो बच्चा सूख गया था उसके पेट में। तो जब उसने दूसरा निकाह किया और मर की मुह पहुँचा तो फिर बच्चे को हरकत हुई और बड़ा हो गया। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने इसकी तस्दीक़ की और निकाह तोड़ डाला तो फ़रमाया के ख़ैर हुई तुम्हारी, कोई बुरी बात मुझे नहीं पहुँची और लड़के का नसब पहले ख़ावंद से साबित किया।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1422" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن امیہ سے روایت ہے کہ ایک عورت کا خاوند مر گیا تو اس نے چار مہینے دس دن تک عدت کی پھر دوسرے شخص سے نکاح کر لیا ابھی اس کے پاس ساڑھے چار مہینے رہی تھی کہ ایک لڑکا جنا حاصا پورا تو اس کا خاوند حضرت عمر کے پاس آیا اور اس نے یہ حال بیان کیا حضرت عمر نے اپنی پرانی عورتوں کو جاہلیت کے زمانے میں تھیں بلوایا اور ان سے پوچھا ان میں سے ایک عورت بولی میں تم کو اس عورت کا حالات باتی ہوں یہ حاملہ ہوگئی تھی اپنے پہلے خاوند سے جو مر گیا تو حیض کا خون بچے پر پڑتے پڑتے وہ بچہ سوکھ گیا تھا اس کے پیٹ میں تو جب اس نے دوسرا نکاح کیا مر کی منہ پہنچے سے پھر بچے کو حرکت ہوئی اور بڑا ہو گیا حضرت عمر نے اس کی تصدیق کی اور نکاح توڑ ڈالا تو فرمایا کہ خیر ہوئی تمہاری کوئی بری بات مجھے نہیں پہنچی اور لڑکے کا نسب پہلے خاوند سے ثابت کیا،۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1422" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1422]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1422" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1422</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1422 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umayyah se riwayat hai ke ek aurat ka khawand mar gaya toh usne chaar mahine das din tak iddat ki, phir dusre shakhs se nikah kar liya. Abhi uske paas sadhe chaar mahine rahi thi ke ek ladka jana (paida kiya) jo bilkul pura tha. Toh uska khawand Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaya aur usne ye haal bayan kiya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne apni purani auraton ko jo jahiliyat ke zamane mein thin bulwaya aur unse pucha. Un mein se ek aurat boli main tumko is aurat ka haal batati hoon, ye hamila ho gayi thi apne pehle khawand se jo mar gaya, toh haiz ka khoon bache par padte padte wo bacha sookh gaya tha uske pait mein. Toh jab usne dusra nikah kiya aur mar ki muh pahuncha toh phir bache ko harkat hui aur bada ho gaya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne iski tasdeeq ki aur nikah tod dala toh farmaya ke khair hui tumhari, koi buri baat mujhe nahi pahunchi aur ladke ka nasab pehle khawand se sabit kiya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1422" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1408

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1423)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1423]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1423</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1423 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، كَانَ يُلِيطُ أَوْلاَدَ الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ بِمَنِ ادَّعَاهُمْ فِي الإِسْلاَمِ فَأَتَى رَجُلاَنِ كِلاَهُمَا يَدَّعِي وَلَدَ امْرَأَةٍ فَدَعَا عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ قَائِفًا فَنَظَرَ إِلَيْهِمَا فَقَالَ الْقَائِفُ لَقَدِ اشْتَرَكَا فِيهِ فَضَرَبَهُ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ بِالدِّرَّةِ ثُمَّ دَعَا الْمَرْأَةَ فَقَالَ أَخْبِرِينِي خَبَرَكِ فَقَالَتْ كَانَ هَذَا - لأَحَدِ الرَّجُلَيْنِ - يَأْتِينِي ‏.‏ وَهِيَ فِي إِبِلٍ لأَهْلِهَا فَلاَ يُفَارِقُهَا حَتَّى يَظُنَّ وَتَظُنَّ أَنَّهُ قَدِ اسْتَمَرَّ بِهَا حَبَلٌ ثُمَّ انْصَرَفَ عَنْهَا فَأُهْرِيقَتْ عَلَيْهِ دِمَاءٌ ثُمَّ خَلَفَ عَلَيْهَا هَذَا - تَعْنِي الآخَرَ - فَلاَ أَدْرِي مِنْ أَيِّهِمَا هُوَ قَالَ فَكَبَّرَ الْقَائِفُ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ لِلْغُلاَمِ وَالِ أَيَّهُمَا شِئْتَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The supplied adjudication episode remains one report around an explicit narrator gloss; identities not printed by the source remain unresolved. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Sulayman ibn Yasar that Umar ibn al-Khattab used to attach the children of the Jahiliyya to whoever claimed them in Islam. Two men came and each of them claimed a woman&#x27;s child. Umar ibn al-Khattab summoned a person who scrutinized features and he looked at them. The scrutinizer said, &quot;They both share in him.&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab hit him with a whip. Then he summoned the woman, and said, &quot;Tell me your tale.&quot; She said, &quot;It was this one</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> who used to come to me while I was with my people&#x27;s camels. He did not leave me until he thought and I thought that I was pregnant. Then he left me, and blood flowed from me, and this other one took his place. I do not know from which of them the child is.&quot; The scrutinizer said, &quot;Allah is greater.&quot; Umar said to the child, &quot;Go to whichever of them you wish</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1423]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1423]</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1423 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसार से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) जाहिलियत के बच्चों को जो उनका दावा करता इस्लाम के ज़माने में उसी से मिला देते। एक बार दो आदमी दावा करते हुए आए एक लड़के का, हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने क़ाएफ़ (नसब पहचानने वाले) को बुलाया। क़ाएफ़ ने देख कर कहा इस लड़के में दोनों शरीक हैं। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने क़ाएफ़ को दर्रे से मारा, फिर उस औरत को बुलाया और कहा तू अपना हाल मुझसे कह। उसने एक मर्द की तरफ इशारा करके कहा के ये मेरे पास आता था और मैं अपने लोगों के ऊंटों में होती थी तो वो मुझसे अलग नहीं होता था बल्कि मुझसे चिमटा रहता था यहाँ तक के वो भी और मैं भी गुमान करते हमल रह जाने का, फिर वो जाता और मुझे ख़ून आया करता तब दूसरा मर्द आता वो भी सोहबत करता, मैं नहीं जानती इन दोनों में से ये किसका नुत्फ़ा है। क़ाएफ़ ये सुन कर ख़ुशी के मारे फूल गया। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा लड़के से तुझे इख़्तियार है जिसे चाहे इन दोनों में से मोलात कर ले।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر جاہلیت کے بچوں کو جو ان کا دعوی کرتا اسلام کے زمانے میں اسی سے ملا دیتے ایک بار دو آدمی دعوی کرتے ہوئے آئے ایک لڑکے کا حضرت عمر نے قائف کو بلایا قائف نے دیکھ کر کہا اس لڑکے میں دونوں شریک ہیں حضرت عمر نے قائف کو درے سے مارا پھر اس عورت کو بلایا اور کہا تو اپنا حال مجھ سے کہہ اس نے ایک مرد کی طرف اشارہ کر کے کہا کہ یہ میرے پاس آتا تھا اور میں اپنے لوگوں کے اونٹوں میں ہوتی تھی تو وہ مجھ سے الگ نہیں ہوتا تھا بلکہ مجھ سے چمٹا رہتا تھا یہاں تک کہ وہ بھی اور بھی بھی گمان کرتے حمل رہ جانے کا پھر جاتا اور مجھے خون آیا کرتا تب دوسرا مرد آتا وہ بھی صحبت کرتا میں نہیں جانتی ان دونوں میں سے یہ کسی کا نطفہ ہے قائف یہ سن کر خوشی کے مارے پھول گیا حضرت عمر نے کہا لڑکے سے تجھے اختیار ہے جس سے چاہے ان دنوں میں سے مولات کر لے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1423</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1423 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) jahiliyat ke bachon ko jo unka da&#x27;wa karta Islam ke zamane mein usi se mila dete. Ek baar do aadmi da&#x27;wa karte hue aaye ek ladke ka, Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Qa&#x27;if (nasab pehchanne wale) ko bulaya. Qa&#x27;if ne dekh kar kaha is ladke mein dono shareek hain. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Qa&#x27;if ko darre se mara, phir us aurat ko bulaya aur kaha tu apna haal mujhse keh. Usne ek mard ki taraf ishara karke kaha ke ye mere paas aata tha aur main apne logon ke unton mein hoti thi toh wo mujhse alag nahi hota tha balki mujhse chimta rehta tha yahan tak ke wo bhi aur main bhi guman karte hamal reh jane ka, phir wo jata aur mujhe khoon aaya karta tab dusra mard aata wo bhi sohbat karta, main nahi janti in dono mein se ye kiska nutfa hai. Qa&#x27;if ye sun kar khushi ke mare phool gaya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha ladke se tujhe ikhtiyar hai jisse chahe in dono mein se molat kar le.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1409

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، أَوْ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ قَضَى أَحَدُهُمَا فِي امْرَأَةٍ غَرَّتْ رَجُلاً بِنَفْسِهَا وَذَكَرَتْ أَنَّهَا حُرَّةٌ فَتَزَوَّجَهَا فَوَلَدَتْ لَهُ أَوْلاَدًا فَقَضَى أَنْ يَفْدِيَ وَلَدَهُ بِمِثْلِهِمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ وَالْقِيمَةُ أَعْدَلُ فِي هَذَا إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The source expressly gives the judge as “Umar ibn al-Khattab or Uthman ibn Affan”. This unresolved alternative is preserved; neither identity is asserted as certain. Later compiler preference remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he had heard that Umar ibn al-Khattab or Uthman ibn Affan gave a judgement about a slave woman who misled a man about herself and said that she was free. He married her and she bore children. It was decided that he should ransom his children with their like of slaves.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1424]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1424" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1424</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1424 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;To ransom them with their price is more equitable in this case, Allah willing</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1424" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>

## Narration 1410

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1425)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1425]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1425" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1425</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1425 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ مَا بَالُ رِجَالٍ يَطَئُونَ وَلاَئِدَهُمْ ثُمَّ يَعْزِلُوهُنَّ لاَ تَأْتِينِي وَلِيدَةٌ يَعْتَرِفُ سَيِّدُهَا أَنْ قَدْ أَلَمَّ بِهَا إِلاَّ أَلْحَقْتُ بِهِ وَلَدَهَا فَاعْزِلُوا بَعْدُ أَوِ اتْرُكُوا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1425" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that Malik related from Ibn Shihab from Salim ibn Abdullah ibn Umar from his father that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;What&#x27;s the matter with men who have intercourse with their slave- girls and then dismiss them? No slave-girl comes to me whose master confesses that he has had intercourse with her but that I connect her child to him, whether or not he has practised coitus interruptus or stopped having intercourse with her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1425]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1425" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1425</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1425 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;The way of doing things generally agreed upon in our community in the case of a man who dies and has sons and one of them claims, &#x27;My father confirmed that so-and- so was his son,&#x27; is that the relationship is not established by the testimony of one man, and the confirmation of the one who confirmed it is only permitted as regards his own share in the division of his father&#x27;s property. The one testified for is only given his due from the share of the testifier.&quot; Malik said, &quot;An example of this is that a man dies leaving two sons, and 600 dinars. Each of them takes 300 dinars. Then one of them testifies that his deceased father confirmed that so-and-so was his son. The one who testifies is obliged to give 100 dinars to the one thus connected. This is half of the inheritance of the one thought to be related, had he been related. If the other confirms him, he takes the other 100 and so he completes his right and his relationship is established. His position is similar to that of a woman who confirms a debt against her father or her husband and the other heirs deny it. She must pay to the person whose debt she confirms, the amount according to her share of the full debt, had it been confirmed against all the heirs. If the woman inherits an eighth, she pays the creditor an eighth of his debt. If a daughter inherits a half, she pays the creditor half of his debt. Whichever women confirm him, pay him according to this. Malik said, &quot;If a man&#x27;s testimony is in agreement with what the woman testified to, that so- and-so had a debt against his father, the creditor is made to take an oath with one witness and he is given all his due. This is not the position with women because a man&#x27;s testimony is allowed and the creditor must take an oath with the testimony of his witness, and take all his due. If he does not take an oath, he only takes from the inheritance of the one who confirmed him according to his share of the debt, because he confirmed his right and the other heirs denied it. It is permitted for him to confirm it.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1425" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ صَفِيَّةَ بِنْتِ أَبِي عُبَيْدٍ، أَنَّهَا أَخْبَرَتْهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ قَالَ مَا بَالُ رِجَالٍ يَطَئُونَ وَلاَئِدَهُمْ ثُمَّ يَدَعُوهُنَّ يَخْرُجْنَ لاَ تَأْتِينِي وَلِيدَةٌ يَعْتَرِفُ سَيِّدُهَا أَنْ قَدْ أَلَمَّ بِهَا إِلاَّ قَدْ أَلْحَقْتُ بِهِ وَلَدَهَا فَأَرْسِلُوهُنَّ بَعْدُ أَوْ أَمْسِكُوهُنَّ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي أُمِّ الْوَلَدِ إِذَا جَنَتْ جِنَايَةً ضَمِنَ سَيِّدُهَا مَا بَيْنَهَا وَبَيْنَ قِيمَتِهَا وَلَيْسَ لَهُ أَنْ يُسَلِّمَهَا وَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ أَنْ يَحْمِلَ مِنْ جِنَايَتِهَا أَكْثَرَ مِنْ قِيمَتِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1426" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi that Safiyya bint Abi Ubayd informed him that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;What is the matter with men who have intercourse with their slave-girls and then leave them to go? No slave-girl comes to me whose master confesses that he has had intercourse with her but that I connect her child to him, whether or not he has practised coitus interruptus or left off from intercourse with her.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1426]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1426" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1426</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1426 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about an umm walad who commits a crime is that her master is liable for what she has done up to her value. He does not have to surrender her, and he cannot be made to bear more than her value for her crime</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1426" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सफ़ियाह बिन उबैद से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फ़रमाया क्या हाल है लोगों को के जमा&#x27; करते हैं अपनी लौंडियों से फिर उनको छोड़ देते हैं, वो निकली फिरती हैं। अब मेरे पास जो लौंडी आएगी और मौला का इक़रार होगा उससे सोहबत करने का तो मैं उसके लड़के का नसब मौला से साबित कर दूंगा। अब इसके बाद चाहे उन्हें भेजा करो चाहे रोके रखा करो। कहा मालिक ने, उम्म-ए-वलद जब जिनायत करे तो मौला उसका तवान दे और उम्म-ए-वलद को इस जिनायत के एवज़ में नहीं दे सकता मगर क़ीमत से ज़्यादा तवान न देगा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1426" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">صفیہ بن عبید سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر نے فرمایا کیا حال ہے لوگوں کو جماع کرتے ہیں اپنی لونڈیوں سے پھر ان کو چھوڑ دیتے ہیں وہ نکلی پھرتی ہیں اب میرے پاس جو لونڈی آئے گی اور مولیٰ کا اقرار ہوگا اس سے صحبت کرنے کا تو میں اس کے لڑکے کا نسب مولیٰ سے ثابت کردوں گا اب اس کے بعد چاہے انہیں بھیجا کرو چاہے روکے رکھا کرو کہا مالک نے ام ولد جب جنایت کرے تو مولیٰ اس کا تاوان دے اور ام ولد کو اس جنایت کے عوض میں نہیں دے سکتا مگر قیمت سے زیادہ تاوان نہ دے گا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1426" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Safiyah bin Ubaid se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya kya haal hai logon ko ke jama&#x27; karte hain apni laundiyon se phir unko chhod dete hain, wo nikli phirti hain. Ab mere paas jo laundi aayegi aur maula ka iqrar hoga usse sohbat karne ka toh main uske ladke ka nasab maula se sabit kardunga. Ab iske baad chahe unhein bheja karo chahe roke rakha karo. Kaha Malik ne, umm-e-walad jab jinayat kare toh maula uska tawan de aur umm-e-walad ko is jinayat ke ewaz mein nahi de sakta magar qeemat se zyada tawan na dega.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1426" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1412

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1427)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ أَحْيَا أَرْضًا مَيِّتَةً فَهِيَ لَهُ وَلَيْسَ لِعِرْقٍ ظَالِمٍ حَقٌّ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1427" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1427</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1427 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If anyone revives dead land, it belongs to him, and the unjust root has no right.&quot; Malik explained, &quot;The unjust root is whatever is taken, or planted without right</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1427" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उरवह बिन ज़ुबैर से रिवायत है के फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने आबाद करे वो उसी की है. जो शख्स ज़ुल्म से वहां कुछ तसर्रुफ़ करे उसको कुछ हक़ नहीं है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1427" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن زبیر سے روایت ہے کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے جو شخص بنجر زمین کو آباد کرے وہ اسی کی ہے جو شخص ظلم سے وہاں کچھ تصرف کرے اس کو کچھ حق نہیں ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>

<p dir="ltr">Urwah bin Zubair se riwayat hai ke farmaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne, jo shakhs banjar zameen ko aabad kare wo usi ki hai. Jo shakhs zulm se wahan kuch tasarruf kare usko kuch haq nahi hai.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ مَنْ أَحْيَا أَرْضًا مَيِّتَةً فَهِيَ لَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Salim ibn Abdullah from his father that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;Whoever revives dead land, it belongs to him.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1428]</p>

## Narration 1414

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1429)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ فِي سَيْلِ مَهْزُورٍ وَمُذَيْنِبٍ ‏ &quot;‏ يُمْسَكُ حَتَّى الْكَعْبَيْنِ ثُمَّ يُرْسِلُ الأَعْلَى عَلَى الأَسْفَلِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1429" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1429</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1429 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm that he heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said about the flood-channels of Mahzur and Mudhaynib (in Madina), &quot;Dam them systematically, so that the water is diverted into each property in turn up to ankle level, starting upstream</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1429" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1429</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1429 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्ला बिन अबी बक्र से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया दो नालों में से, एक का नाम महरोज़ था और दूसरे का नाम मुज़ानिब, के जिसका बाग़ नाले के मुत्तसिल है वो अपने बाग़ में टखनों-टखनों पानी भर के फिर दूसरे के बाग़ में पानी छोड़ दे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1429" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن ابی بکر سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا دونالوں میں ایک کا نام مہروز تھا اور دوسرے کا نام مذینیب کہ جس کا باغ نالہ کے متصل ہے وہ اپنے باغ میں ٹخنوں ٹخنوں پانی بھر کے پھر دوسرے کے باغ میں پانی چھوڑ دے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يُمْنَعُ فَضْلُ الْمَاءِ لِيُمْنَعَ بِهِ الْكَلأُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1430]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1430" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1430]</p>

## Narration 1416

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1431)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الرِّجَالِ، مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنْ أُمِّهِ، عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ أَنَّهَا أَخْبَرَتْهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يُمْنَعُ نَقْعُ بِئْرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1431]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1431" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1431</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1431 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Abu&#x27;r-Rijal Muhammad ibn Abd ar-Rahman from his mother Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that she informed him that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not withhold the surplus water of a well from people</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1431]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1431" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1431]</p>

## Narration 1417

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1432)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ يَحْيَى الْمَازِنِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ ضَرَرَ وَلاَ ضِرَارَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1432]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1432" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 1418

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1433)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَمْنَعُ أَحَدُكُمْ جَارَهُ خَشَبَةً يَغْرِزُهَا فى جِدَارِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ يَقُولُ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ مَا لِي أَرَاكُمْ عَنْهَا مُعْرِضِينَ وَاللَّهِ لأَرْمِيَنَّ بِهَا بَيْنَ أَكْتَافِكُمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1433</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1433 ]</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;No one should prevent his neighbour from fixing a wooden peg in his wall.&quot; Then Abu Hurayra said, &quot;Why do I see you turning away from it? By Allah! I shall keep on at you about it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने: मन करते तुम में से कोई अपने हमसाया को लकड़ी गाड़ने से अपनी दीवार में. फिर अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे: क्या वजह है के तुम इस हदीथ को मुतवज्जा हो कर नहीं सुनते? क़सम अल्लाह की</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کرتے تم میں سے کوئی اپنے ہمسایہ کو لکڑی گاڑنے سے اپنی دیوار میں پھر ابوہریرہ کہتے تھے کیا وجہ ہے کہ تم اس حدیث کو متوجہ ہو کر نہیں سنتے قسم اللہ کی میں اس کو خوب مشہور کروں گا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke farmaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne: Mana karte tum mein se koi apne hamsaya ko lakdi gaadne se apni deewar mein. Phir Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte the: Kya wajah hai ke tum is hadith ko mutawajja ho kar nahi sunte? Qasam Allah ki, main is ko khoob mashhoor karunga.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1419

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1434)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1434]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1434" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1434</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1434 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ يَحْيَى الْمَازِنِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ الضَّحَّاكَ بْنَ خَلِيفَةَ، سَاقَ خَلِيجًا لَهُ مِنَ الْعُرَيْضِ فَأَرَادَ أَنْ يَمُرَّ بِهِ فِي أَرْضِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مَسْلَمَةَ فَأَبَى مُحَمَّدٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ الضَّحَّاكُ لِمَ تَمْنَعُنِي وَهُوَ لَكَ مَنْفَعَةٌ تَشْرَبُ بِهِ أَوَّلاً وَآخِرًا وَلاَ يَضُرُّكَ ‏.‏ فَأَبَى مُحَمَّدٌ فَكَلَّمَ فِيهِ الضَّحَّاكُ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ فَدَعَا عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ مُحَمَّدَ بْنَ مَسْلَمَةَ فَأَمَرَهُ أَنْ يُخَلِّيَ سَبِيلَهُ فَقَالَ مُحَمَّدٌ لاَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ لِمَ تَمْنَعُ أَخَاكَ مَا يَنْفَعُهُ وَهُوَ لَكَ نَافِعٌ تَسْقِي بِهِ أَوَّلاً وَآخِرًا وَهُوَ لاَ يَضُرُّكَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ مُحَمَّدٌ لاَ وَاللَّهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ وَاللَّهِ لَيَمُرَّنَّ بِهِ وَلَوْ عَلَى بَطْنِكَ ‏.‏ فَأَمَرَهُ عُمَرُ أَنْ يَمُرَّ بِهِ فَفَعَلَ الضَّحَّاكُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1434" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Reported irrigation dispute and Umar&#x27;s ruling; preserve the participants&#x27; dialogue as a Companion report, without Prophetic attribution.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Amr ibn Yahya al-Mazini from his father that ad-Dahhak ibn Khalifa watered his irrigation ditch from a large source of water. He wanted to have it pass through the land of Muhammad ibn Maslama, and Muhammad refused. Ad-Dahhak said to him, &quot;Why do you prevent me? It will benefit you. You can drink from it first and last and it will not harm you.&quot; Muhammed refused so ad- Dahhak spoke about it to Umar ibn al-Khattab, and Umar ibn al-Khattab summoned Muhammad ibn Maslama and ordered him to clear the way. Muhammad said, &quot;No.&quot; Umar said, &quot;Why do you prevent your brother from what will benefit him and is also useful for you? You will take water from it first and last and it will not harm you.&quot; Muhammad said, &quot;No, by Allah!&quot; Umar said, &quot;By Allah, he will pass it through, even if it is over your belly!&quot; Umar ordered him to allow its passage and ad-Dahhak did so</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1434]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1434" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">याहया बिन अमराह से रिवायत है के दाहक बिन खलीफा ने एक नहर निकली अर्ज़ में से में से हो कर. उन्होंने मना किया. दाहक ने कहा तुम क्यों मन करते हो? तुम्हारा तो इस में नफा है अव्वल और आखिर पानी दिया करना और कुछ ज़रर नहीं. मुहम्मद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने न मन. दाहक ने हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से बयां किया. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने मुहम्मद बिन मसलमा को बुला कर कहा: तुम इजाज़त दो. मुहम्मद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: मैं न दूंगा. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: तुम अपने भाई मुसलमान को ऐसी बात से मना करते हो जिस में उसका नफ़ा है और तुम्हारा भी नफा है? तुम भी पानी लिया करना अव्वल और आखिर में और तुम्हारा कुछ ज़रर नहीं. मुहम्मद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: क़सम अल्लाह की (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: वह नहर बहायी जाये अगरचे तुम्हारे पेट पर से हो. फिर हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने दाहक को हुकुम किया नहर जारी करने का मुहम्मद बिन मसलमा की ज़मीन से हो कर. दाहक ने ऐसा ही किया.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1434" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحیی بن عمارہ سے روایت ہے کہ ضحاک بن خلیفہ نے ایک نہر نکالی عرض میں سے محمد بن مسلمہ کی زمین میں سے ہو کر انہوں نے منع کیا ضحاک نے کہا تم کیوں منع کرتے ہو تمہارا تو اس میں نفع ہے اپنی زمین کو اول اور آخر پانی دیا کرنا اور کچھ ضرر نہی محمد نہ مانا ضحاک نے حضرت عمر سے بیان کیا حضرت عمر نے محمد بن مسلمہ کا بلا کر کہا تم اجازت دو محمد نے کہا میں نہ دوں گا حضرت عمر نے کہا تم اپنے بھائی مسلمان کو ایسی بات سے منع کرتے ہو جس میں اس کا نفع ہے اور تمہارا بھی نفع ہے تم بھی پانی لیا کرنا اول اور آخر میں اور تمہارا کچھ ضرر نہیں محمد نے کہا قسم اللہ کی میں اجازت نہ دونگا حضرت عمر نے کہا وہ نہر بہائی جائے اگرچہ تمہارے پیٹ پر سے ہو پھر حضرت عمر نے ضحاک کو حکم کیا نہر جاری کرنے گا محمد بن مسلمہ کی زمین سے ہو کر ضحاک نے ایسا ہی کہا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1434" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>
